*Ahmed M. Nor,Lukas T. N. Landau,E. Veronica Belmega,Rodrigo C. de Lamare*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出了一种低复杂度的基于用户满意度的MU-MIMO调度算法（US-SA），通过构建低维子分组矩阵和动态消除已满足用户，在接近最优性能的同时大幅降低计算开销，并在多项指标上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多用户多输入多输出（MU-MIMO）系统中的调度对于高效资源分配和整体性能提升至关重要。现有方法在解决组合优化问题时面临计算复杂度高的挑战，特别是在大规模资源块（RB）场景下穷举搜索（EX）不可行，且需要在最大化系统性能的同时保证用户公平性。

Method: 提出了一种基于用户满意度的调度方法（US-SA）。该方法在每个帧构建低维子分组矩阵，用于在每个时隙调度最优多用户组；已被满足的用户将从调度过程中移除，从而降低计算复杂度。

Result: US-SA在满意度、传输数据量、频谱效率、延迟和公平性方面接近最优穷举搜索（EX）方法，同时具有更低的计算成本。实验结果表明，所提出的方案优于现有竞争技术。

Conclusion: US-SA是一种高效的MU-MIMO多用户调度方案，通过低维子分组矩阵和满意度驱动的用户消除机制，在保持近最优性能的同时显著降低了计算复杂度，在满意度、频谱效率和公平性等关键指标上均表现出色。

Abstract: Scheduling in multiuser multiple input multiple output (MU-MIMO) systems is essential for efficient resource allocation and overall performance enhancement. In this work, a multiuser scheduling problem is formulated to maximize the product of user equipments' (UEs) aggregate satisfactions, which maintains user fairness. Solving such a combinatorial problem using exhaustive search (EX), which requires evaluating all possible multiuser groups within a massive number of resource blocks (RBs), is prohibitive. Instead, we propose an efficient users' satisfaction based scheduling approach (US-SA). In our US-SA, a low dimension sub-grouping matrix is constructed {at each frame}, which is used to schedule the best multiuser group in each time slot; satisfied users are eliminated from the scheduling process. Our US-SA performs close to the optimal EX method in terms of satisfaction, transmitted data amount, spectral efficiency, latency, and fairness with lower computational cost. Moreover, our experiments demonstrate that the proposed scheme outperforms competing techniques.

</details>


### [89] [New Approximations of Non-Separable MIMO Channels by Separable Channels for Accurate Ergodic Capacity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15427)
*Thanh Luan Nguyen,Zygmunt J. Haas,Chadi Abou-Rjeily,Georges Kaddoum*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对Weichselberger MIMO信道模型的分析复杂性，提出了基于KLD的最近可分离近似和基于矩匹配的Wishart分布映射两种方法，实现了闭式遍历容量表达，其中矩匹配方法在各SNR下均优于传统Kronecker模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Weichselberger信道模型在下一代无线应用中的MIMO系统中因高精度表征不可分离信道而获得广泛采用，但其不可分离结构引入了严重的分析复杂性，导致文献中缺乏可处理的数学框架，亟需进一步研究。

Method: 1) 在Kullback-Leibler散度(KLD)下推导Weichselberger模型最近的可分离（双相关瑞利）衰落模型，该问题等价于Itakura-Saito(IS)距离准则下的秩-1非负矩阵分解；2) 提出一种新的矩匹配方法(MMM)，将精确信道统计映射到Wishart分布的统计特性。

Result: 1) 高SNR渐近分析表明，KLD近似比传统Kronecker模型获得更紧致的容量估计，尤其在稀疏和非规则散射环境中；2) KLD模型因无法保持总信道功率而在低SNR下存在容量表征缺陷；3) MMM模型在所有SNR范围内均一致性地改善了传统Kronecker模型的容量精度；4) 两种方法均可直接利用遍历容量的精确闭式表达式。

Conclusion: 通过KLD和MMM两种方法，成功构建了Weichselberger不可分离信道模型的可分离近似框架，有效解决了分析复杂性问题。其中MMM方法在各SNR范围内表现更为稳健，为下一代无线MIMO系统的信道建模与容量分析提供了实用的数学工具。

Abstract: In recent years, owing to the high accuracy in characterizing non-separable channels prevalent in next-generation wireless applications, the classical Weichselberger channel model has gained widespread adoption in multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems. However, its non-separable structure also introduces severe analytical complexity, leading to a lack of tractable mathematical frameworks in the literature and thus raises an urgent need for further research. To address the aforementioned analytical complexity, we first derive the nearest separable (double-correlated Rayleigh) fading model to the Weichselberger model under the Kullback-Leibler divergence (KLD), a problem equivalent to rank-1 nonnegative matrix factorization under the Itakura-Saito (IS) distance criterion. The results of our asymptotic analysis in the high-SNR regime reveal that the KLD-enabled approximation achieves a tighter capacity estimate than the conventional Kronecker model, especially in sparse and non-regular scattering environments. Yet, a key limitation of the KLD-enabled model is its tendency to mischaracterize the channel capacity in the low-SNR regime due to its inability to preserve total channel power. As a more robust alternative, we introduce a novel moment matching method (MMM) aimed at mapping the exact channel statistics to those of a Wishart distribution. Both the KLD-enabled and MMM-enabled separable channel directly enable the use of exact closed-form expressions for the ergodic capacity. Numerical results demonstrate that the MMM-enabled model consistently improves upon the capacity accuracy of the conventional Kronecker model across all SNR regimes.

</details>


### [90] [Sparse Port Selection under Mutual Coupling in Fluid Antenna Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15599)
*Jingyuan Xu,Haoyu Liang,Zaichen Zhang,Jian Dang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对流体天线系统中密集排布天线候选位置时的互耦问题，提出了一种互耦感知波束赋形框架，将完整天线晶格建模为耦合多端口网络，通过耦合电压-场响应选择激活端口及其源电压。数值结果表明，该方法相比忽略互耦的设计和固定阵列，能同时提升平均主瓣信噪比和峰值旁瓣电平，证明互耦应在设计阶段被主动利用而非仅在最终评估中补偿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体天线系统通过在有限区域内重构天线位置获取空间自由度，可用远少于候选位置的射频馈电合成赋形波束。然而，当候选位置密集排布时，电磁互耦会改变终端电压、感应电流与辐射场之间的关系，导致非耦合模型无法准确描述硬件特性，可能激活不合适的端口集合，从而扭曲合成方向图。因此，需要建立考虑互耦效应的系统设计框架。

Method: 1) 将期望波束幅度转换为与有限孔径兼容的复目标；2) 将完整天线晶格建模为耦合多端口网络；3) 通过耦合电压-场响应选择激活端口及其源电压；4) 非激活候选端口仍作为网络的一部分携带感应电流；5) 所有对比设计在相同电磁模型和相同源电压预算下进行评估。

Result: 数值结果表明，互耦感知设计相比忽略互耦的端口选择和固定阵列，同时改善了平均主瓣信噪比(SNR)和峰值旁瓣电平(PSLL)，验证了互耦效应应在设计阶段被主动利用，而非仅在最终评估阶段进行补偿。

Conclusion: 本文提出的互耦感知框架为密集流体天线系统的波束赋形提供了有效解决方案。核心结论是：电磁互耦不应被视为需要消除或事后补偿的负面因素，而是应当在设计阶段被主动利用以优化系统性能。该方法通过统一电磁模型下的公平比较，证明了考虑互耦的联合端口选择与激励优化能够显著提升波束质量指标。

Abstract: Fluid antenna systems obtain spatial degrees of freedom by reconfiguring antenna positions within a confined region, a principle that extends to beamforming: shaped beams can be synthesized using far fewer radio-frequency feeds than candidate antenna positions. When the candidates are densely arranged, however, electromagnetic mutual coupling changes the relationship among terminal voltages, induced currents, and radiated fields, so an uncoupled model no longer describes the hardware and may activate an unsuitable set of ports, distorting the synthesized pattern. This paper develops a mutual-coupling-aware framework that converts the desired beam amplitude into a finite-aperture-compatible complex target and models the complete antenna lattice as a coupled multiport network, selecting the active ports and their source voltages through the coupled voltage-to-field response. Inactive candidate ports remain part of the network and carry induced currents, and every compared design is evaluated through the same electromagnetic model under the same source-voltage budget. Numerical results show that the mutual-coupling-aware design improves both the average mainlobe signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) and the peak sidelobe level (PSLL) over coupling-unaware selection and a fixed array, demonstrating that mutual coupling should be exploited in the design itself rather than compensated only in the final evaluation.

</details>


### [91] [Off-Grid Position Optimization under Mutual Coupling in Fluid Antenna Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15603)
*Jingyuan Xu,Jian Dang,Zaichen Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种电磁感知（EM-aware）波束成形框架，用于平面流体天线阵列，通过联合优化天线位置和激励电流，在独立扰动的对称双波束目标下，相比均匀阵列和离散端口选择，持续提升平均主瓣信噪比（SNR）并降低峰值旁瓣电平（PSLL）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体天线阵列通过在有限孔径内连续重定位天线获得超越网格约束端口选择的几何多样性，但每次位移会同时改变辐射响应和多端口互阻抗网络，将几何优化与源电压约束耦合在一起。现有方法未能有效处理这种电磁耦合效应，因此需要建立电磁感知的联合优化框架。

Method: 本文提出了一种电磁感知波束成形框架，包含三个核心步骤：（1）相位恢复——将仅幅度的赋形波束指标转换为与孔径兼容的复数目标；（2）EM感知正交匹配追踪（OMP）——选择网格约束的初始天线位置；（3）连续精化——交替进行精确电压约束电流优化与运动约束的投影自适应矩估计（Adam）更新所有物理天线位置。

Result: 在独立扰动的对称双波束目标下，所提方法相比均匀阵列和离散端口选择，能够持续提升平均主瓣信噪比（SNR）并降低峰值旁瓣电平（PSLL）。

Conclusion: 本文提出的电磁感知波束成形框架通过联合优化天线连续位置和激励电流，有效解决了流体天线阵列中几何优化与电磁耦合的耦合问题，为平面流体天线阵列的高性能波束成形提供了系统化的解决方案。

Abstract: Fluid antenna arrays exploit continuous antenna repositioning within a finite aperture to provide geometry diversity beyond grid-constrained port selection. Every displacement, however, changes both the radiation response and the multiport mutual-impedance network, coupling geometry optimization with the source-voltage constraint. This paper develops an electromagnetic-aware (EM-aware) beamforming framework for planar fluid antenna arrays. Phase retrieval converts an amplitude-only shaped-beam specification into an aperture-compatible complex target, and an EM-aware orthogonal matching pursuit (OMP) method selects grid-constrained initial antenna positions. Continuous refinement then alternates exact voltage-constrained current optimization with movement-constrained projected adaptive moment estimation (Adam) updates of all physical antenna positions. Across independently perturbed symmetric dual-beam targets, the proposed method consistently improves the average mainlobe signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) and reduces the peak sidelobe level (PSLL) over a uniform array and discrete port selection.

</details>


### [92] [Time- and Space-Efficient List Decoding up to Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15937)
*Dorsa Fathollahi,Noga Ron-Zewi,Mary Wootters*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文构造了首个逼近容量的列表可解码码，实现了确定性近线性时间（N^{1+τ}）和次线性空间（N^τ）的列表解码算法，输出列表大小和字母表大小均为常数，结果可扩展至容量可实现的列表可恢复码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 列表解码（list-decoding）是纠错码理论中的核心问题，旨在给定接收字后找出所有与接收字距离不超过ρ的码字。虽然已有达到容量的列表可解码码及其快速近线性时间算法，但现有工作大多未考虑空间复杂度。近期Cook和Moshkovitz（2024-2026）开创了低空间确定性算法的研究，但他们2026年的工作中构造的码远离容量。因此，设计同时满足时间效率、空间效率和容量逼近的确定性列表解码算法成为亟待解决的问题。

Method: 本文构造了逼近容量的列表可解码码，实现了确定性的时间和空间高效列表解码算法。具体而言，对于任意速率R∈(0,1)和任意小常数τ>0，构造了速率R的码族，能够在半径ρ=1-R-τ下进行确定性列表解码。

Result: 对于任意R∈(0,1)和任意小常数τ>0，构造了码族C⊆Σ^N，速率R，确定性列表解码半径达到ρ=1-R-τ（逼近Singleton界和容量的最优权衡），解码时间为N^{1+τ}（近线性时间），空间复杂度为N^τ（次线性空间），输出列表大小和字母表大小均为常数。结果还可扩展到达到容量的列表可恢复码（list-recoverable codes）。

Conclusion: 本文首次实现了同时逼近容量、具有确定性近线性时间算法和次线性空间复杂度的列表可解码码，填补了Cook和Moshkovitz工作中码率远离容量的空白。该成果在时间和空间效率上均达到最优或近最优，并可扩展至列表可恢复码设定，为低空间复杂度下的纠错码算法设计提供了重要突破。

Abstract: In the theory of error correcting codes, list-decoding refers to the following problem. Given a code $C \subseteq Σ^N$ and a received word $y \in Σ^N$, find all codewords $c \in C$ so that $δ(c,y) \leq ρ$, where $δ$ is relative Hamming distance and $ρ\in (0,1)$. Codes that approach the optimal trade-off between the rate $R := \log_{|Σ|}(|C|) / N$ and the list-decoding radius $ρ$ are said to achieve capacity.By now, there are constructions of capacity-achieving list-decodable codes with fast near-linear-time list-decoding algorithms, but most existing work has not considered space complexity.
  In a recent line of work, Cook and Moshkovitz (2024, 2025, 2026) initiated the study of low-space deterministic algorithms for error correcting codes. In particular, in their 2026 paper, they gave a construction of list-decodable codes with deterministic near-linear-time and sublinear space list-decoding algorithms. However, these codes were far from achieving capacity.
  In this paper, we present list-decodable codes approaching capacity with deterministic time- and space-efficient list-decoding algorithms. More precisely, for any $R \in (0,1)$ and any arbitrarily small constant $τ> 0$, we present a family of codes $C\subseteq Σ^N$ with rate $R$ that are deterministically list-decodable up to radius $ρ= 1 - R - τ$, in time $N^{1 + τ}$ and space $N^τ$ with constant output list size and constant alphabet size. Our results can be extended to capacity-achieving list-recoverable codes.

</details>


### [93] [Adaptive Unequal Error Protection for Semantic Split Learning over Wireless Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16227)
*Vukan Ninkovic,Dejan Vukobratovic,Dragisa Miskovic,Chao Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向无线边云推理的任务感知语义分割学习框架，通过互信息驱动的非均匀差错保护动态调整潜在表示的传输可靠性，实验表明其在各信噪比条件下均优于均匀保护和固定非均匀保护基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无线边云推理场景中，分割学习(SL)需要在边缘设备与云端之间传输潜在表示(latent representations)。传统方法对所有潜在分量采用同等保护，忽略了不同分量对下游任务的贡献差异，导致通信资源利用效率低下。因此，需要一种能够根据任务相关性动态调整传输可靠性的机制，以优化边云协作推理性能。

Method: 1. 构建基于自编码器(AE)的物理层，实现通信接口的端到端学习；2. 采用互信息(MI)估计量化各潜在分量与下游任务输出的关联程度；3. 利用MI估计值对各分量的梯度作为任务敏感性的代理指标；4. 基于MI驱动优先级实现非均匀差错保护(UEP)，在物理层将更高发射功率分配给任务关键分量；5. 整个框架以完全数据驱动的方式自适应数据分布和下游任务。

Result: 1. 在真实物联网感知数据上的实验表明，所提框架在各信噪比(SNR)条件下均优于均匀差错保护(EEP)和固定非均匀差错保护基线；2. MI引导的UEP显著提高了任务关键潜在分量的发射功率分配；3. 排名稳定性分析验证了优先级排序的一致性；4. MI估计器展现出良好的鲁棒性；5. 框架在不同数据集和任务类型上具有良好的泛化能力，表明其广泛的适用性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的任务感知语义分割学习框架通过互信息驱动的非均匀差错保护，实现了通信可靠性与任务相关性的动态适配。该方法完全基于学习驱动，无需人工设计优先级规则，能够自适应数据分布和下游任务需求。实验验证了其在真实场景中的性能优势和泛化能力，为无线边云协作推理中的语义通信提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a task-aware semantic split learning (SL) framework for wireless edge-cloud inference, in which the reliability of transmitted latent representations is dynamically adapted to their relevance for the downstream task. An autoencoder (AE)-based physical (PHY) layer enables end-to-end learning of the communication interface, while unequal error protection (UEP) is realized via mutual information (MI)-driven prioritization of latent components during training. The gradient of the estimated MI with respect to each latent component serves as a sensitivity-based proxy for task relevance, providing a fully learning-driven prioritization that adapts to both the data distribution and the downstream task. We further show that this prioritization translates into measurable physical-layer effects: MI-guided UEP assigns significantly higher transmit power to the most task-critical latent components compared to the equal error protection (EEP) baseline. Experiments on real-world IoT sensing data demonstrate consistent gains over equal and fixed-UEP baselines across SNR regimes. Additional analysis confirms ranking stability, estimator robustness and generalization across datasets and task types, indicating broad applicability of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [94] [Maximal correlation under cardinality constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16397)
*Dror Drach,Tomer Berg,Or Ordentlich,Ofer Shayevitz*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文定义并分析了量化最大相关（quantized maximal correlation），通过将其与特定线性组合的MMSE量化失真关联，结合率失真技术和反集中不等式，得到了可张量化的维度无关上界，并改进了可逆马尔可夫链的等周常数界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最大相关（maximal correlation）虽然信息论中重要，但对取值空间无限制。实际应用中常需处理有限取值（量化）的函数，因此需要研究受限基数下的量化最大相关；此外，经典结果如Alon-Milman关于马尔可夫链等周常数的界仍有改进空间。

Method: 1) 建立量化函数相关性与特定随机变量线性组合量化MMSE失真之间的联系；2) 利用率失真理论技术约束该MMSE；3) 应用反集中不等式（anti-concentration inequalities）进一步收紧界；4) 利用所得MMSE界的张量化性质处理乘积分布的高维情形。

Result: 1) 获得了量化最大相关的显式上界；2) 该上界具有维度无关性（dimension-free），适用于乘积分布；3) 改进了可逆马尔可夫链及乘积链的等周常数（isoperimetric constants）上界，强化了Alon-Milman等经典结果。

Conclusion: 量化最大相关虽本身不具有张量化性质，但通过转化为MMSE量化问题，可导出可张量化的显式界。该框架不仅解决了高维乘积分布的量化相关分析，还为马尔可夫链的等周问题提供了更紧的界，展示了率失真方法与概率不等式在信息论和组合问题中的强大结合。

Abstract: In this paper, we define and analyze the quantized maximal correlation, an extension of the notion of maximal correlation restricted to functions taking values in sets of bounded cardinality. We derive an upper bound on the quantized maximal correlation by showing that the correlation between any quantized functions of $X$ and $Y$ is related to the MMSE distortion in quantization of a particular linear combination of random variables. Following this, we leverage rate-distortion techniques and anti-concentration inequalities to further bound this MMSE, which results in explicit bounds on the quantized maximal correlation. Unlike the quantized maximal correlation itself, which does not generally tensorize, our bounds on the mean squared error do tensorize, resulting in a dimension-free upper bound on the quantized maximal correlation for product distributions. Our results also lead to improved bounds on the isoperimetric constants of reversible Markov chains and product chains, strengthening classical results such as those by Alon and Milman.

</details>


### [95] [Revisiting Shannon's Source Coding Theorem with Distributional Uncertainty under the Nonlinear Expectation Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16543)
*Wen-Xuan Lang,Shaoshi Yang,Jianhua Zhang,Zhiming Ma*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 基于非线性期望理论，将经典信息熵推广为非线性信息熵，建立了不确定分布信源的非线性信源编码定理，证明非线性信息熵是可达编码率的上界，拓展了经典信息论处理分布不确定场景的适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典信息论假设信源可由单一精确已知的概率分布建模，但实际通信网络中存在大量不可预期、非平稳且异质的随机事件，确定性概率模型过于理想化。因此，需要放松这一假设，刻画信源消息分布本身的不确定性。

Method: 基于非线性期望理论（一种扩展经典概率论的新公理化体系），研究分布本身不确定的信源（称为"不确定分布信源"）；将经典信息熵推广为非线性信息熵；利用次线性期望下的强大数定律建立非线性信源编码定理。

Result: 1) 提出非线性信息熵，用于度量不确定分布信源所含信息量；2) 建立非线性信源编码定理：非线性信息熵是不确定分布信源在最大错误概率准则下可达编码率下确界的上界；3) 确定了不确定分布信源在最小错误概率准则下编码率的聚点。

Conclusion: 引入非线性期望理论能够更全面地理解信息源，为处理实际通信网络中概率分布不确定的复杂场景提供了理论基础，将经典信息论拓展至分布不确定的广义框架。

Abstract: In classical information theory, a source is modeled by a single, precisely known probability distribution. However, in the increasingly complex communication networks full of unanticipated, nonstationary, and heterogeneous random events, the assumption of precise and well-defined probability distributions to describe random variables appears somewhat idealized. Therefore, it is important to characterize the uncertainty of distributions of source messages, subject to relaxing the assumption of deterministic probability models for analyzing information sources in information theory. Based on the nonlinear expectation theory, a novel axiomatical system that extends classical probability theory, this paper investigates the information sources whose distributions themselves are uncertain, and refers to them as uncertain-distribution sources. We generalize the fundamental concept information entropy to nonlinear information entropy, which describes the measurement of the amount of information contained in a uncertain-distribution source. By using the strong law of large numbers under sublinear expectation, we establish a nonlinear source coding theorem, which not only shows that the nonlinear information entropy is the upper bound for the infimum of achievable coding rate of uncertain-distribution sources under the maximum error probability criterion, but also determines a cluster point of the coding rate of uncertain-distribution sources under the minimum error probability criterion. Our findings reveal that the introduction of nonlinear expectation theory allows for a more comprehensive understanding of information sources.

</details>


### [96] [Cramér-Rao Bound Analysis for Cell-Free ISAC Systems with Fluid Intelligent Metasurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16634)
*Changhao He,Asmaa Abdallah,Ahmed M. Eltawil*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种流体智能超表面（FIM）增强的无蜂窝集成感知与通信（ISAC）架构，通过分布式接入点（AP）的多角度观测充分利用FIM的形态灵活性。推导了目标角度估计的完整Fisher信息矩阵和闭式定位CRB，揭示了三种无蜂窝特有现象：跨AP信息耦合、收发FIM乘性耦合和角度分集放大。提出的交替优化算法实现了联合波束赋形与FIM形状设计，28GHz配置下相比单AP固定阵列基线可获得4.5dB的CRB降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FIM辅助ISAC研究依赖共址单基站部署，由于观测角度受限，未能充分利用FIM的形态灵活性。流体智能超表面的连续物理重构能力在受限视角下无法发挥最优无线性能，亟需通过分布式架构释放其潜力。

Method: 1) 建立FIM增强的无蜂窝ISAC架构，分布式AP从多角度协同观测目标；2) 推导目标角度估计的完整Fisher信息矩阵；3) 获得显式量化角度分集增益的闭式定位Cramér-Rao界（CRB）；4) 分析Fisher信息矩阵的块结构，揭示三种无蜂窝特有现象；5) 提出基于半定松弛的交替优化算法进行联合波束赋形与FIM形状设计，并形式化证明其紧性。

Result: 1) 理论发现：跨AP信息耦合、收发FIM乘性耦合、角度分集放大三种现象；2) 28GHz配置（4个AP，每AP 8个FIM单元）下，分布式角度分集将FIM形态增益放大至15.8dB，而同等总天线数的单AP对部署仅0.4dB；3) 在10dB感知SNR下，所提架构相比单AP固定阵列基线实现4.5dB定位CRB降低；4) 在整个帕累托前沿上保持通信服务质量约束。

Conclusion: FIM增强的无蜂窝ISAC架构通过分布式多角度观测显著放大了流体超表面的形态增益，突破了单基站部署的视角限制。所提出的联合优化框架在感知精度与通信质量之间实现了有效权衡，为下一代通感一体化网络提供了新的架构范式。

Abstract: Fluid intelligent metasurface (FIM) is an emerging antenna architecture that continuously reshapes its physical geometry to optimize wireless performance. While existing studies on FIM-aided integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) rely on co-located single-base-station (BS) deployments, they fundamentally underutilize FIM's morphological flexibility due to restricted observation angles. In this paper, we investigate a FIM-augmented cell-free ISAC architecture, where distributed access points (APs) collaboratively observe a target from diverse angles. We derive the complete Fisher information matrix for target angle estimation and obtain a closed-form localization CRB that explicitly quantifies the angular diversity gain. By analyzing the block structure of the Fisher information matrix, we uncover three cell-free-specific phenomena: (i) cross-AP information coupling, (ii) multiplicative Tx--Rx FIM coupling, and (iii) angular diversity amplification. Under a 28\,GHz configuration with four APs and eight FIM elements per AP, our analysis shows that distributed angular diversity amplifies the FIM morphing gain to 15.8\,dB, compared to only 0.4\,dB in a single-AP pair deployment with the same total antenna count. We further propose an alternating optimization algorithm for joint beamforming and FIM shape design via semidefinite relaxation whose tightness is formally proved. Numerical results confirm that the proposed cell-free FIM-ISAC architecture achieves a 4.5\,dB localization CRB reduction over the single-AP fixed-array baseline at 10\,dB sensing SNR while maintaining communication quality-of-service constraints across the entire Pareto frontier.

</details>


### [97] [Two-Level Decorrelated Coded Modulation on the $D_4$ Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16662)
*Leopold Bertholet,Chloe Makdad,Stephen Mackes,Daniel Chew,Matthew Robinso*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: TLDC是一种面向D4格点的新型低复杂度编码调制方案，通过两级解码实现变量去相关，支持并行处理与灵活FEC选择，在16-QAM短块长传输下可获得0.5 dB性能增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于D4格点的编码调制方案在实现格点整形和编码增益时复杂度较高，且第二级变量间存在相关性限制了并行解码性能。需要一种低复杂度且能充分利用编码增益的格点编码调制方案。

Method: 提出两级去相关编码(TLDC)：1) 结合Voronoi整形与两级解码过程的编码调制方案；2) 利用第一级解码值使第二级的多个随机变量近似去相关；3) 第二级变量的独立性支持并行解码或合并为更大码字；4) 每级可灵活选择FEC方案（如比特交织极化码或多级极化码）。

Result: 在AWGN信道下，16-QAM调制、块大小为64和1024比特时，相比同类最先进编码调制方案可获得最高0.5 dB的增益；同时保持了低复杂度特性并支持灵活的FEC选择。

Conclusion: TLDC通过两级去相关机制有效平衡了性能与复杂度，为D4格点编码调制提供了一种实用的低复杂度解决方案，在短块长场景下展现出显著性能优势。

Abstract: We propose \textit{two-level decorrelated coding} (TLDC), a novel coded modulation scheme for the $D_4$ lattice that combines Voronoi shaping with a two-stage decoding process to achieve lattice shaping and coding gains at low complexity. In TLDC, the decoded values of the first level allow the several random variables in the second level to become approximately uncorrelated. The resulting independence of the variables in level two permits decoding in parallel or consolidation into a larger codeword, enhancing performance. TLDC supports flexible choice of FEC within each level. Using bit-interleaved or multi-level polar codes at each level, the resulting coded modulation scheme exhibits a gain of up to 0.5 dB over analogous state-of-the-art coded modulation schemes on a 16-QAM under AWGN at block sizes of 64 and 1024 bits.

</details>


### [98] [Non-Binary Quasi-Cyclic LDPC Codes with Entanglement Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16761)
*Pavan Kumar,Shayan Srinivasa Garani*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了两类基于有限域的非二进制纠缠辅助准循环量子LDPC码构造方法，通过精心的图设计在纠错性能与纠缠消耗之间取得平衡，其中第一类方案仅需单个Bell对即可实现纠缠辅助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错码是实现可靠量子通信和量子计算的关键技术。非二进制量子LDPC码相比二进制码具有更好的纠错性能，而纠缠辅助可以突破传统量子码的CSS构造限制，构造出更优的码。准循环结构便于硬件实现。然而，如何在最小化纠缠资源消耗的同时保证良好的纠错性能（如消除Tanner图中的短环）是一个重要挑战。

Method: 提出了两类构造方法：（1）第一类基于一对非二进制经典QC-LDPC码，使得所得EA-QC-QLDPC码的非辅助部分的Tanner图无4-环，且仅使用单个Bell对建立收发端纠缠；（2）第二类基于单个非二进制经典QC-LDPC码，其Tanner图本身无4-环。两类构造均在任意有限域上实现，且码率可精确确定。

Result: 成功构造了两类具有精确码率的非二进制EA-QC-QLDPC码，实现了Tanner图的无4-环特性，其中第一类方案将纠缠资源消耗降至最低（仅需单个Bell对），证明了基于图的精心设计能够有效平衡纠错性能与纠缠消耗。

Conclusion: 通过图论指导的码构造方法，可以在有限域上实现高效的非二进制纠缠辅助准循环量子LDPC码，在最小化纠缠资源的前提下获得良好的纠错性能，为实际量子通信系统提供了实用的编码方案。

Abstract: We construct two families of non-binary entanglement assisted (EA) quasi-cyclic (QC) quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) codes over arbitrary finite fields, each possessing a precisely determined code rate.
  The first family is derived from a pair of non-binary classical QC-LDPC codes, designed such that the unassisted portion of the overall Tanner graph of the resulting EA-QC-QLDPC code is free of 4-cycles. The second family, on the other hand, is constructed from a single non-binary classical QC-LDPC code whose Tanner graph itself is 4-cycle-free. In developing the codes belonging to the first family, we employ a \emph{single Bell pair} to establish entanglement between the transmitter and the receiver, thereby minimizing the required entanglement resources. Furthermore, these constructions demonstrate that careful graph-based design can effectively balance error-correction performance with entanglement consumption, providing a practical approach for realizing efficient non-binary EA-QC-QLDPC codes.

</details>


### [99] [Fluid Antenna Array-Inspired Location-Posterior-Driven Subarray Sizing and Power Control for Two-Hop AF UAV Relaying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16770)
*Xuanyi Zhu,Jian Dang,Chen Zhao,Huaifeng Shi,Zaichen Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于流体天线阵列的子阵列尺寸与功率联合优化方法，通过利用位置后验分布的几何可重构性，在位置不确定性下平衡增益与波束鲁棒性，实现了UAV两跳AF中继的显著功耗节省和服务成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机（UAV）中继通信系统中，用户位置存在不确定性，传统的固定阵列设计难以在阵列增益与波束指向鲁棒性之间取得平衡。流体天线阵列（FAA）提供了几何可重构性，但如何自适应地控制有效孔径以应对位置不确定性下的两跳AF中继传输仍缺乏有效方案。

Method: 提出了一种基于FAA启发的子阵列尺寸与发射功率联合设计方法。核心方法包括：（1）利用渐进收缩的用户位置后验分布来跟踪位置不确定性；（2）将位置协方差投影到阵列方向上，导出与方向不确定性成反比的闭式方向受限尺寸；（3）通过后验样本传播计算两跳速率模型；（4）以最小化UAV功耗为目标，在满足最差用户速率下尾约束和硬件功率限制的条件下，联合优化子阵列尺寸和发射功率；（5）在真实用户位置处的瞬时两跳Rician信道上对规划配置进行审计验证。

Result: 在t=8s时刻，所提设计在成对可行几何结构上相比全阵列窄波束传输节省3.17 dB功耗；在0.15 W功耗预算下，服务成功率达到60.0%，而固定八单元子阵列仅为43.2%。

Conclusion: 通过自适应控制可重构子阵列的有效孔径，所提方法能够利用几何可重构性在阵列增益与指向鲁棒性之间实现最优权衡，显著提升了位置不确定性环境下UAV AF中继系统的功率效率和服务可靠性。

Abstract: This paper develops fluid antenna array (FAA)-inspired subarray sizing and transmit-power design for a two-hop amplify-and-forward (AF) unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) relay using progressively contracting user-location posteriors. A contiguous reconfigurable subarray is shared by first-hop reception and second-hop forwarding, such that its active size jointly determines the receive gain, forwarding gain, and beamwidth. By adaptively controlling the effective aperture, the proposed design exploits geometric reconfigurability to balance array gain against pointing robustness under location uncertainty. Projecting the position covariance onto the array direction yields a closed-form direction-limited size inversely proportional to directional uncertainty. Posterior samples are propagated through the two-hop rate model, and the subarray size and transmit power are then selected to minimize UAV power subject to a worst-user lower-tail rate requirement and hardware power limits. The planned configuration is further audited over instantaneous two-hop Rician channels at the true user positions. At t = 8 s, the proposed design saves 3.17 dB over full-array narrow-beam transmission on paired feasible geometries and achieves 60.0% service success at a 0.15-W budget, compared with 43.2% for a fixed eight-element subarray.

</details>


### [100] [Sample Complexity of Peer Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16838)
*Abdellah Aznag,Robin Bowers,Rachel Cummings,Jason Hartline,Matthew vonAllmen,Bo Waggoner*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了同伴预测（peer prediction）中互信息函数的无偏估计器问题。主要发现：3个及以下样本时唯一可估计的互信息是零；4-5个二元报告样本时行列式互信息（DMI）是唯一的（至多标量倍）；6个样本时DMI不再唯一。作者还提出了改进的DMI估计器，分析了随机样本数估计器（包括"stop-short"估计器）以及基于评分规则的互信息类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同伴预测旨在通过奖励联合报告集合来激励智能体真实报告观测信号，而无需观察真实值。研究的核心问题是：对于给定数量的样本，哪些互信息函数存在无偏估计器？这直接关系到设计有效的同伴预测机制，以在缺乏真实值的情况下激励真实报告。

Method: 1. 基于Kong和Schoenebeck(2019)的广义信息论互信息定义，将互信息定义为非负且对所有信息结构都不鼓励混淆报告的联合分布函数；2. 分析不同样本数量下互信息无偏估计器的存在性和唯一性；3. 对二元报告空间的4-5个样本情况，证明DMI的唯一性；4. 构造改进的DMI估计器并分析其收敛率；5. 研究随机样本数估计器（特别是"stop-short"估计器）和基于评分规则的互信息类别。

Result: 1. 3个及以下样本的报告对：唯一具有无偏估计器的互信息是平凡的零函数；2. 4-5个样本且二元报告空间：行列式互信息（DMI）是唯一的互信息（至多标量倍数）；3. 6个样本时：DMI不再唯一；4. 提供了适用于任意样本数量的改进DMI估计器，并刻画了其收敛速率；5. 证明了"stop-short"估计器（先验有界随机样本数）可以比等效固定样本估计器获得更低方差；6. 在基于评分规则的互信息族中，识别出期望样本数低于3即可估计的互信息。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了不同样本约束下互信息估计的可能性边界：固定少量样本（≤3）无法有效估计非平凡互信息，4-5个二元样本时DMI具有独特优势，但6个样本后唯一性消失。随机化样本数量（特别是stop-short策略）和基于评分规则的方法为突破样本限制提供了新途径，为同伴预测机制设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Peer prediction seeks to incentivize agents to truthfully report an observed signal by rewarding joint sets of reports without observing a ground truth. Following the generalization of information-theoretic mutual information introduced in Kong and Schoenebeck (2019), we call a function of a joint distribution over signals a mutual information when it is non-negative and disincentivizes garbling reports for all information structures. An unbiased estimator for a mutual information takes some number of samples from the distribution and returns rewards for both agents, such that the expected reward is equal to the mutual information. We seek to characterize the set of mutual informations with unbiased estimators for a given number of samples.
  We show that for three or fewer sampled report pairs, the only mutual information with an unbiased estimator is trivially zero, and for four or five samples with a binary report space, the Determinant Mutual Information (DMI) of Kong (2024) is the unique mutual information (up to a scalar multiple). We further show that DMI ceases to be unique at six samples. We provide an improved estimator of DMI for any given number of samples and characterize its convergence rate.
  We also examine mutual information estimators that accept a randomized number of samples. First, we show that mutual information estimators on an ex-ante bounded number of samples (termed "stop-short estimators") can achieve a lower variance than an equivalent fixed-sample estimator (for DMI). Second, we introduce the class of scoring-rule-based mutual informations and identify in this family a mutual information that can be estimated with under three samples in expectation.
